# 🔥 Project Reforged (former HD Patch) 🔥

## Post 148026 by Stormhand81 (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2025-08-24T15:14:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=148026#p148026 | page 1 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

****![⚙️](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/2699.svg) Project Reforged — Official Launch****

After a long period of refinement, testing, and iteration,
what started as a collection of HD assets and scattered patches
has evolved into something much more cohesive.

 ****Project Reforged is now live.****

What once required hunting down multiple sources,
manual integration, and constant troubleshooting
is now unified into a **single, curated system**.

 ---

 **![🎯](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f3af.svg) What it does**

• Cinematic lighting and color
• Modern depth and visual clarity
• Smoother and more stable performance
• Fully integrated pipeline (ReShade + DXVK)

All built to enhance the game **without breaking its original identity.**

 ---

 **![🔗](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f517.svg) Full project page (downloads & details):**
 **<https://projectreforged.github.io/>**

 ---

 **![🔒](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f512.svg) Premium System**

The project now includes a premium tier with:

• Fully optimized presets
• Advanced DXVK configurations
• Experimental builds
• Direct support

 ---

 **![💬](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f4ac.svg) Discord (updates, support, community):**
<https://discord.gg/jnvkayMbqJ>

 ---

This is no longer a loose collection of mods.
It’s a **refined system designed to work as a whole.**

If you’ve followed this project before —
this is the version everything was leading to.

— Stormhand

## Post 148649 by Allwynd01 (Bug Report Enthusiast) — 2025-08-27T12:51:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=148649#p148649 | page 2 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

Good job, looking at the Ashenvale screenshot, I can't tell it looks any different than Retail WoW. Personally such graphics updates aren't my thing, but I appreciate the good work.

I noticed some other user posting about some NPC Dialog addon that makes it look more modern and he was using some HD patch and the screenshots were in Elwynn Forest. While thinks looked more detailed, one thing I noticed is that the patch entirely changed the look of Elwynn Forest, but not in a good way.

It made everything look much darker. The textures for road, grass, tree bark, tree crowns, everything looks like a much darker shade of the original color. For me this changes the zone's feeling entirely. The original Elwynn Forest for me is supposed to be much more brighter and vibrant. I'm guessing this uses textures from Retail, I don't know why they made such a change, but it looks like an entirely different zone that's pretending to be Elwynn Forest.

Is this common for other zones with the HD patch? Because I don't think it's very good. I'm OK with more detailed textures, but changing the colors do darker or brighter changes the feeling of a zone completely.

This is not a complaint, just an observation.

Hello! Thanks for updating this fantastic mod. I saw in the changelog a few updates ago that there was a fix to undead causing crashes. I'm not sure if this was in the scope of that fix but just thought I'd report it anyway because it's still happening. I've been experiencing visual glitches, freezes and crashes when fighting this undead mob throughout the game. This has been happening for months, even in the older Patch A by Falarson. It's with this specific undead model (using generic image of of the mob's non-HD model, but it happens with any mob that uses this model):

![Image](https://preview.redd.it/classic-death-knights-v0-ley5vgc566ke1.jpg?width=320&crop=smart&auto=webp&s=909065caa99be35d21fecddfd8d97e99c42c135b)

I'll have to get it on video next time because I don't know how else to explain it. Black lines suddenly appear from the model and go across my screen, and when it does, my game freezes for a few seconds. If it happens too many time in a short span of time my game will outright crash. Any time I run Strat or Karazhan I have to disable the patch first. This is really the last source of consistent crashes I'm experiencing with this patch, so I hope you're able to figure out a fix! Thanks again.

<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=151342#p151342 | page 6 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

> **Apav wrote: Thu Sep 11, 2025 2:43 pm**
> Hello! Thanks for updating this fantastic mod. I saw in the changelog a few updates ago that there was a fix to undead causing crashes. I'm not sure if this was in the scope of that fix but just thought I'd report it anyway because it's still happening. I've been experiencing visual glitches, freezes and crashes when fighting this undead mob throughout the game. This has been happening for months, even in the older Patch A by Falarson. It's with this specific undead model (using generic image of of the mob's non-HD model, but it happens with any mob that uses this model):
>
>  ![Image](https://preview.redd.it/classic-death-knights-v0-ley5vgc566ke1.jpg?width=320&crop=smart&auto=webp&s=909065caa99be35d21fecddfd8d97e99c42c135b)
>
>  I'll have to get it on video next time because I don't know how else to explain it. Black lines suddenly appear from the model and go across my screen, and when it does, my game freezes for a few seconds. If it happens too many time in a short span of time my game will outright crash. Any time I run Strat or Karazhan I have to disable the patch first. This is really the last source of consistent crashes I'm experiencing with this patch, so I hope you're able to figure out a fix! Thanks again.

This is really helpful. I'm always keeping that issue in mind, we will figure it out at some point.

PS: I remember fighting this mob near the lake in Silverpine forest, with my undead warlock. Casting Drain Soul included. No crashes. I don't know, do you have any other mods installed?

I am using (in this load order):
Your Patch A
Your Patch B
Patch C: Auto-attack weapon sound update by redmagejoe
Patch E: Blood Enhanced by Vidooo
Patch F: Fog Pushback by Vidooo
Patch H: Alt HD - HD Tilesets, ground clutter, frill doodads, trees, bushes, etc by Vidooo

On the Alt HD thread it specifically says "Patch-B from Turtle HD adds an abundance of building and doodad textures that go very well with this mod" but this was for the old Patch B, I don't know if that's still true with yours but I haven't had any other issues with using both.

Got a video example:

[embed: //streamable.com/e/y7lymz]

This time it was flashes of bright light instead of black lines, this is not a spell effect. But you can see the little microstutters when they happen. If there were more of those mobs causing more of those split second freezes, my game would've crashed. This seems to be happening with both color variants of the mob you see in the video.

Edit: Did a Part 2 of someone else fighting the mob because it was a bit heavier on the freezes, plus a bug I never experienced before happened with my entire screen going blank for a second.

[embed: //streamable.com/e/f6x6j0]

Glad to know, dxvk can be tough. I'm using a sarek/async edition, in my opinion, much more stable and performance friendly.

As for the mod file size: there is something about MPQ - when you edit (add/delete/change files), everything is kept there. Meaning lots of residue, and empty spaces. When you compact using a mpq editor, those "holes" just vanish, and you get the file its real size. Even CLASSIIC HD mod author didn't know that (I just told him hehe)... so we have a much smaller file size now, because of that ;)

## Post 151940 by Bytor — 2025-09-16T13:20:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=151940#p151940 | page 8 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

**Issue: Chimaera Roost Vale (Feralas) – Patch B**

While exploring the Chimaera Roost Vale in Feralas with the latest Patch B, I encountered invisible walls.

 **Bug Behavior:**

Attempting to jump 'through' the walls may result in getting stuck mid-air.

A GM was unable to unstuck me and had to teleport my character to Darnassus.

This issue may generate unnecessary GM workload in the future.

The questing zone in Chimaera Vale is currently inaccessible with this patch.

Disabling only Patch B eliminates the issue.

 **Additional Note:**

The WDB folder is being deleted on every client launch.

 **Images:**

 1. Invisible walls
![Image](https://i.ibb.co/Z1SM8Nfv/Untitled.png)

 2. Stuck mid-air
![Image](https://i.ibb.co/Gv7hBcC6/image.png)

## Post 152892 by Tnewel — 2025-09-23T23:18:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=152892#p152892 | page 11 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

So I have loved patch A-B-M from this release. However the author has refused to elaborate on potential threats encountered by Windows Defender and Webpage redirection. I will also say Patch B has a lot of issues. Unless you are strictly a Roleplayer, the enhancement patches do in fact impact the playability of the the game. I often get stuck on world environmental and required GM assistance to become un-stuck. Also certain quests are broken due to the patch. One such instance is Agamand Heirlooms which is an Undead warrior quest in Brill that leads to the Agamand family crypt.

## Post 152902 by Stormhand81 (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2025-09-24T02:46:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=152902#p152902 | page 11 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

> So I have loved patch A-B-M from this release. However the author has refused to elaborate on potential threats encountered by Windows Defender and Webpage redirection. I often get stuck on world environmental and required GM assistance to become un-stuck. Also certain quests are broken due to the patch. One such instance is Agamand Heirlooms which is an Undead warrior quest in Brill that leads to the Agamand family crypt.

 1. I am not the author - even if I were, I never heard about Windows Defender and Webpage redirection issues;
 2. Yes Patch-B still has issues, and it will continue to have for long time, sorry to disappoint ;)
 3. You can always disable patch-B if you need to complete a quest that is broken by the patch.

I am doing the best that I can to mantain this, and to fix all issues I encounter and those that are reported - some are more difficult to figure out/fix, but that doesn't mean I am not listening.

Thanks.

>  I am doing the best that I can to mantain this, and to fix all issues I encounter and those that are reported - some are more difficult to figure out/fix, but that doesn't mean I am not listening.
>
>  Thanks.

I just wanted to say keep the fantastic job Stormhand81. Sure, there are some issues here and there, but overall the game experience is so much better thanks to your work. Please keep it up!
And honestly, since Patch B isn’t mandatory, I don’t really understand why some people complain so strongly. If it causes problems for specific quests, it can just be disabled temporarily.

## Post 154735 by Donchujano — 2025-10-07T15:17:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154735#p154735 | page 14 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Hello, I’d like to report a consistent problem I’ve been having since Patch A (with Vish’s Vanilla textures) was merged.

Issue: The game always crashes right after login, when the character selection screen loads.

Details:

WDB and Cache folders have been cleared.

When I disable all other patches and run only Patch A → crash still happens.

When I disable Patch A and run all other patches (B, C, M, O, older HD assets from E, plus the login screen Patch X) → the game works perfectly.

So it’s not a conflict between patches – everything works together, except Patch A.

Crash behavior:

The game launches fine, I can reach the login screen.

After entering credentials → crash at the character menu.

If I try 10 times, eventually it works once, and once I’m inside the game it runs flawlessly for hours without issues.

It’s completely random – sometimes it loads on the first attempt, sometimes only after 10 tries.

I’m honestly lost at this point. Patch A seems to be the cause ( Even on it's own  separatly it does not work), and it looks like it might be related to how the game loads large textures into RAM/VRAM on such an old client.

Could you please check if there’s a way to update Patch A to avoid this texture loading crash for some users?

Thank you, i love your hard work and i would really love to remain use your patches, because to be honest it's one of mine greatest experiences i have from playing wow right now.

## Post 154806 by Candleman — 2025-10-08T06:01:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154806#p154806 | page 14 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> RESHADE INSTALLATION GUIDE
>  - Download latest Reshade: <https://reshade.me/>
>  - Download DXVK (doitsujin) 2.6 (IT DOES NOT WORK ON ANY VERSIONS ABOVE IT): <https://github.com/doitsujin/dxvk/relea> ... 2.6.tar.gz;
>  - Download my personal DXVK.CONF here: <https://www.mediafire.com/file/2yjiaw0e> ... .conf/file.
>
>  - Install reshade pointing to WoW.exe, select DIRECTX9, and make sure you pick all shaders and effects.
>  - Copy d3d9.dll from DXVK downloaded folder (pick the one from x32 folder) and REPLACE the one installed by reshade in your WoW game folder;
>  - Copy my preset file (INI) to the same main game folder;
>  - Copy my custom dxvk.conf into your game folder;
>  - Open game, make sure reshade is installed by pressing HOME KEY.

If I replace the d3d9.dll with the one from DXVK I cannot open Reshade with the Home Button anymore. Nothing happens. If I dont replace the dll and use the reshade original, I can open Reshade, but I don't see any shader effects.

I followed your install by the book. Any ideas?

>
>  - Install reshade pointing to WoW.exe, select DIRECTX9, and make sure you pick all shaders and effects.

>  - Open game, make sure reshade is installed by pressing HOME KEY.

>  I was also curious if it's possible to make day time darker over all? As someone with migraines, a number of zones are painfully bright. My monitors already run low brightness, but it'd be nice to be able to play more during the day without avoiding the dry, sandy, bright zones.

I don't think the text is HD patch related. You can use lots of addons for that.

I recommend using Reshade for adjusting not only day time, but lots of visual settings.

Hello Stormhand81, thank you for your efforts and sharing. I am a player on the SEA server. I wonder if the EU and SA servers do not have this donate mount. In my game, this mount has always been a white kodo model without textures.

![Image](https://i.imgur.com/EwfDRTj.png)
original ![Image](https://i.imgur.com/7AfzTBc.jpeg)

The English name of this mount should be "Arcane Furline", and the first kodo mount in the top-left corner of the list should be "Sunwarmed Furline". I'm not sure if the EU or SA servers have different models and names for these two items, which is why the cat mount has turned into a kodo.

![Image](https://i.imgur.com/cntSbCI.png)
original ![Image](https://i.imgur.com/aqYuJjz.jpeg)
This also applies to the mount called "Ruin".

There have also been a few times when I seemed to see some cube-shaped helmets with white and purple checkered textures in the city. However, I didn’t take screenshots at the time. I will provide an updated response with details the next time I encounter this issue.

It would be great if this issue could be fixed. Thank you again for your efforts.  satisfied_turtle

Hey again.
I believe the new HD patch might not be working for some of us because of a conflict with the Vanilla Helper mod.
Whenever I have Vanilla Helper enabled, the game freezes at around one quarter of the login loading bar, and then an error window pops up.

In the previous update, the only change I noticed was that my character’s pants disappeared lol, but I still couldn’t log into the game.
![Image](https://i.imgur.com/HcoS23a.jpeg)
With the newest update, the pants issue is fixed, but the login problem is still there.   hiding_smth_turtle_head
![Image](https://i.imgur.com/JfHFzpU.jpeg)

When I try launching the game with only patches A, B, D, and E, the game does load — but I see green textures on many models around me, and HD models are missing.
![Image](https://i.imgur.com/IfkLqt0.jpeg)

Unfortunately, the other patches won’t load without Vanilla Helper, so I can’t properly test them unless I revert back to the older versions of the mods ( the ones without the patch U,L  and everything) — which, by the way, work perfectly fine and I never had any issues with them (ofc beside few glitches but thats fine).

So i tried launching the game with Vanilla Helper and the mentioned old patches, and it immediately crashed, which makes me strongly suspect that the problem comes from Vanilla Helper itself.

I hope this could be just some minor issue, i am fine with the older patches but i am afraid with newest updates it might become difficult to use them. Thank you as always!

## Post 160279 by Ankerdankerf — 2025-11-21T21:59:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=160279#p160279 | page 22 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Stormhand81 wrote: Sun Aug 24, 2025 3:14 pm**
> RESHADE INSTALLATION GUIDE
>  - Download latest Reshade: <https://reshade.me/>;
>  - Download DXVK (doitsujin) 2.6 (IT DOES NOT WORK ON ANY VERSIONS ABOVE IT): <https://github.com/doitsujin/dxvk/relea> ... 2.6.tar.gz;
>  - Download my personal DXVK.CONF here: <https://www.mediafire.com/file/2yjiaw0e> ... .conf/file;
>
>  - Install reshade pointing to WoW.exe, select VULKAN, and make sure you pick all shaders and effects.
>  - Copy d3d9.dll from DXVK downloaded folder (pick the one from x32 folder) copy it into your WoW game folder;
>  - Copy my preset file (INI) to the same main game folder;
>  - Copy my custom dxvk.conf into your game folder;
>  - Open game, make sure reshade is installed by pressing HOME KEY.
>
>  Enjoy the best WoW visuals ever!

Does it exactly but... if I activate ReShade ingame, then the CPU Usage grow up to hell and I just have ~20fps in the LoginScreen, the Game itself is not playable. - And I have no SSAA for transparent textures (but thats a OGL/VK issue)

However, I can't use reshade with dxvk in WoW Vanilla.

After disabling dxvk in the twow launcher(or delete the d3d9.dll) I have ~90-144(VSync) in Stormwind, depents on the location, with active reshade.

## Post 161026 by Ralecgos — 2025-11-28T21:23:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=161026#p161026 | page 23 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Stormhand81 wrote: Fri Nov 28, 2025 11:31 am**
> > **Ralecgos wrote: Fri Nov 28, 2025 6:26 am**
> > Female humans with the HD patches applied for the most part seem to all have 1 dull blackish/brown eye color regardless of which face you choose. As well as very strong dark eyeliner on most faces.
>
>   These hd faces were exact copies of retail textures, I did not edit them at all, just cropped and resize to match vanilla texture size. Eyes are mostly the same, with subtle differences, except for one red and other greenish eye.

Well, that's a shame. I don't like telling people how they should do things, but the HD patch for character models on Ascension WoW private server for example seem to be closer to what retail HD models actually are supposed to look like on the whole. Female human faces are all distinct with eye colors being varied instead of bland, one color, and "wrong" looking. Hairstyles seem to line up between classic models and HD models as well across every race.

Maybe it's an issue with applying HD patches to the very old vanilla WoW client, I guess. But if it was just texture issues here, I'd love to see a fix across all races in the future.

Thanks again for taking the time to read <3.

## Post 161717 by Xithone — 2025-12-05T17:16:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=161717#p161717 | page 24 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Stormhand81 wrote: Fri Nov 28, 2025 11:31 am**
> > **Ralecgos wrote: Fri Nov 28, 2025 6:26 am**
> > Female humans with the HD patches applied for the most part seem to all have 1 dull blackish/brown eye color regardless of which face you choose. As well as very strong dark eyeliner on most faces.
>
>   These hd faces were exact copies of retail textures, I did not edit them at all, just cropped and resize to match vanilla texture size. Eyes are mostly the same, with subtle differences, except for one red and other greenish eye.

Just came to say, was loving the HD stuff until I went to make a human female.  I don't know when faces got updated for retail/TWoW, but my human female actually looks better without the HD mod.  And most of the faces look like the same person.  It made me sad.  Loving the mod for my Tauren girl, but literally rerolled my traditionally human rogue to another race to avoid that face.

Would be nice if the armor textures didn't hinge on using the HD skins.

Edit:  I've just given up on the HD patch for now.  I went back the tried some more stuff, and my chars are getting different hairstyles with and without the patch (I know custom ones change but I expected the HD made ones to revert to the same hairstyle at least.  I'm sure that's TWoW's fault for how they order the options, but still.)  The other thing really driving me away is that my human character is kinda getting whitewashed.  It's a combination of things doing it, not just the texture itself.  But I've taken and compared screenshots without any mods and with the HD stuff, both with and without the character model texture changes, and the skin will always be lighter with more red.  Like the darkest human female skin with the HD mod is equivalent to the 2nd or 3rd darkest without mods, and even then has a more ruddy tone.

*Last edited by Xithone on Fri Dec 05, 2025 6:45 pm, edited 1 time in total.*

## Post 162914 by Magnusthemage — 2025-12-16T13:15:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=162914#p162914 | page 26 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Really loving the recent patches — the HD visuals and updated spell animations add a lot without breaking the classic feel. Great work ![👏](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f44f.svg)

One small thing I was curious about: are there any plans (now or later) to add the Warlock green fire visuals? It’s such an iconic fantasy piece for locks, and it would fit really well alongside the other visual enhancements you’ve already done.

For a bit of context, I’ve tried the community TurtleHD green fire mod here:
<https://github.com/redmagejoe/TurtleHD/>

It shows exactly the kind of green fire effect I’m looking for, but it doesn’t seem to work well with the latest Turtle client (some newer items don’t render correctly). For example, Immolate does show the green fire effect correctly, but spells like Soul Fire and Searing Pain still use the default visuals — ideally I’d love all of them to share the same green fire treatment.

I’m also using the Incubus replacement mod listed on the wiki:
<https://turtle-wow.fandom.com/wiki/Client_Mods>

That one’s already working fine on my end, so green fire would be the bigger priority for me personally if anything like this were ever considered.

Not a request or complaint at all — just sharing what’s already out there and asking if green fire might be something you’d consider for a future patch. Thanks again for all the effort you’re putting into the project!

This has already been said here I think but DON'T put the Patch Manager into the TurtleWoW directory. Put it anywhere else. I just have it on my desktop. Then set the path above in the application to the TurtleWoW folder. Also keep the updater file together with the Patch Manager file in the WOW_HD_Patch_Manager folder. It should work then. I never had any problems with it.

Its a really cool rework.
Sadly I can't use it because it needs dxvk, but with dxvk I cant use MSAA+SSAA(=leafs and grass, hair ect AA) because vulkan don't support that function and ReShade is unuseble on wow with dxvk. the fps go down below 30, often below 20 fps without any used effects(dxvk team say, reshade is the problem, reshade say, dxvk is the problem...), btw, if I turn reshade off I have in most situation something between 80 and 144(capped) fps. with d3d9(without dxvk) also ~80-144fps WITH reshade on and a bunch of effects.
but...higher textures without reshade and without FAA is mutch more ugly for me.

## Post 165062 by Stormhand81 (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2026-01-13T21:11:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=165062#p165062 | page 28 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Hawksley wrote: Tue Jan 13, 2026 7:20 pm**
> I know I'm probably being really dumb but:
>
>  Enable DXVK in the Launcher
>
>  Do you mean the Turtle Wow launcher? I'm guessing not as you specifically make mention of TW launcher in the next line of instruction.
>
>  Edit: Is dxvk.dll supposed to be in the dxvk folder? I can't see it, just x32 and x64 neither of which has dxvk in it.

Updated the instructions:

- Extract DXVK compressed folder
- Open the DXVK folder
- Go to the x32 folder
- Copy d3d9.dll into your World of Warcraft game folder
- Enable DXVK in the Launcher
- Open the Turtle WoW Launcher
- Go to the MODS tab
- Enable DXVK
And yes, launcher is TWOW launcher.

There is no dxvk.dll... only d3d9.dll

> - Open the Turtle WoW Launcher
> - Go to the MODS tab
> - Enable DXVK
>   And yes, launcher is TWOW launcher.
>
>  There is no dxvk.dll... only d3d9.dll

## Post 167041 by Ingameacc12345 (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2026-01-31T12:40:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167041#p167041 | page 30 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Thule wrote: Sat Jan 31, 2026 12:29 pm**
> > **Ingameacc12345 wrote: Thu Jan 29, 2026 10:22 pm**
> > > **Thule wrote: Thu Jan 29, 2026 9:56 pm**
> > > Is somewhere any list of rewritten and changed tracks and sounds of the PATCH-S: Sounds & Music?
> > >  What exactly this patch contains?
> > >  Thanks!
> >
> >   Quite a lot! This is the list of modified files/sounds: <https://pastebin.com/LcgxwbqV>
>
>   Thanks! I wonder if someone could guide me to change some zone music on my own. How can I do that?
>  MPQ editor I guess? Have anyone try that?
>  Thanks in advance.   sad_turtle_head

It is pretty wasy to do and there is zero risk you can damage your installation (make a copy of the **Patch-S.mpq** file just in case). As for tools, you only need **Ladik's MPQ Editor**.

The process is as follows:
 1. Open **Patch-S.mpq** in the MPQ Editor and find the zone you want to change the music for - this is the most difficult part as sometimes it's tricky to find what you want.
 2. Once you locate what you need (you can extract and listen to the file to make sure this is the music you want to replace), just name your own replacement mp3 file in the exact same way and drag/drop it into the MPQ file, replacing the old mp3 file.
![Image](https://i.imgur.com/yTSrIVz.png)
 3. Profit.

## Post 167558 by Schwarzschild (Barrens Chat Casualty) — 2026-02-06T03:07:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167558#p167558 | page 30 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Without me changing anything it now happens more and more often that I log in and my fps won't go beyond ~15, even out in the middle of nowhere. Waiting for 10-20 minutes makes no difference (in case shaders needed to be loaded or so). Tried disabling all addons, no effect. I have ALL the recommended settings, mods, pretty killer PC, updated graphics drivers, etc. Even disabling the Reforged mods makes no difference. The only thing that "helps" is disabling dxvk completely. Disabling dxvk gets my frames up to 100+ immediately, but then I can basically not use Reforged as I get even more crashes than usual and texture glitches. Tried the older dxvk 2.5.3, same thing with fps stuck around 15. Uninstalling and re-installing all mods also had no effect.

Really getting frustrated at this point. Been tinkering around with Reforged & dxvk more than actually playing the game it feels :-(

PS: The solution: I have to close ALL programs in task manager, even if they are not using ANY CPU or RAM. Then my fps return to normal. Must have smth to do with that wretched old shit client bottlenecking the CPU and certain cores being reserved or unavailable by dormant processes or so??? Good job TWoW team wasting years on your Unreal client instead of porting TWoW to a newer WoW client  mad_turtle

I was more opt and willing to donate before you went to this greedy model and literally took away a tool that you provided to us for free.
>
>  regardless thanks for the effort and the patches but this has killed it for me.

I understand your point and I am sorry to hear that.

First, old patcher was not made by me, nor maintained. I had no connection with it. The official patch we have now is.

Second, it is a way of saying "thanks" for supporters - not the opposite, as a punishment for non-supporters. HD patch itself, which is the main thing, is free for all. And it will always be.

Without support, I cannot offer 1/10 of time and dedication to the project. Which means it would probably be abandoned at this point.

The model we have now is a balance in that matter. So we all win in the end. ![😉](//cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f609.svg)

Thalassian Unicorn : sounds are there, but they are sooooo much quieter than any other mount I have, especially compared to the Swift Thalassian Unicorn, it's barely audible.
